} // Check if the text contains spaces, hyphens, or '(' const hasSpaces = text.includes(' '); const hasHyphen = text.includes('-'); const hasParentheses = text.includes('('); const hasSlash = text.includes('/'); if (hasSpaces || hasHyphen || hasParentheses || hasSlash) { // Split the text into parts by spaces and separators const regex = /([\s\-()/]+)/; const parts = text.split(regex); // Process and capitalize words const capitalizedParts = parts.map(part => { if (part.match(/[\s\-()]+/)) { // Retain spaces and separators return part; } else { const lowercasedPart = part.toLowerCase(); if (lowercasedPart.startsWith('mc') && lowercasedPart.length > 2) { // Handle the 'McDonalds' case where the third letter needs to be capitalized return 'Mc' + part.charAt(2).toUpperCase() + lowercasedPart.substring(3); } else { // Check if the part is in a whitelist before transforming return uppercaseWhitelist.includes(part) ? part : whitelist.includes(lowercasedPart) ? lowercasedPart : lowercasedPart.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + lowercasedPart.slice(1); } } }); // Join the processed parts back together return capitalizedParts.join(''); } else { // If there's only one word, capitalize the first letter unless it's in the uppercase whitelist return uppercaseWhitelist.includes(text) ? text : text.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + text.slice(1).toLowerCase(); } } Related Information').text('Historical Records ') .append($(' ', { id: 'historical-position-total-records' }).text(`(${data.length} records)`))) .append($(' ')); let $reloadableDiv = $(' ', { class: 'reloadable-content fade-in' }); $('.cfo-only-button').removeClass('show'); } else { $("#advanced-panel").slideDown("fast"); $(this).attr('aria-expanded', 'true'); $('.cfo-only-button').addClass('show'); } } }); }); function cleanCaps(text) { if (text === null) { return ''; } // Check if the text contains spaces, hyphens, or '(' const hasSpaces = text.includes(' '); const hasHyphen = text.includes('-'); const hasParentheses = text.includes('('); const hasSlash = text.includes('/'); if (hasSpaces || hasHyphen || hasParentheses || hasSlash) { // Split the text into parts by spaces and separators const regex = /([\s\-()/]+)/; const parts = text.split(regex); // Process and capitalize words const capitalizedParts = parts.map(part => { if (part.match(/[\s\-()]+/)) { // Retain spaces and separators return part; } else { const lowercasedPart = part.toLowerCase(); if (lowercasedPart.startsWith('mc') && lowercasedPart.length > 2) { // Handle the 'McDonalds' case where the third letter needs to be capitalized return 'Mc' + part.charAt(2).toUpperCase() + lowercasedPart.substring(3); } else { // Check if the part is in a whitelist before transforming return uppercaseWhitelist.includes(part) ? part : whitelist.includes(lowercasedPart) ? lowercasedPart : lowercasedPart.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + lowercasedPart.slice(1); } } }); // Join the processed parts back together return capitalizedParts.join(''); } else { // If there's only one word, capitalize the first letter unless it's in the uppercase whitelist return uppercaseWhitelist.includes(text) ? text : text.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + text.slice(1).toLowerCase(); } } Related InformationEXCL-BBBDB87496EC21EEFALSESINGLE_SOURCE_REVIEWPROPOSED_NOT_ADMITTED
